DEFINE TABLE memory SCHEMALESS; DEFINE TABLE memory_history SCHEMALESS; CREATE memory:a SET content = "v1", version = 1;
LET $table = 'memory'; LET $key = 'a'; LET $content = 'v2'; BEGIN TRANSACTION;
LET $before = (SELECT content, version FROM ONLY type::record($table, $key));
IF $before = NONE { THROW "memory not found" };
UPDATE type::record($table, $key) SET content = $content, version = $before.version + 1;
INSERT INTO memory_history { memory_id: type::record($table, $key), version: $before.version + 1, old_content: $before.content, new_content: $content, change_type: "updated" };
COMMIT TRANSACTION;
SELECT content, version FROM memory:a; SELECT version, old_content, new_content FROM memory_history;
LET $table = 'memory'; LET $key = 'zzz'; LET $content = 'x'; BEGIN TRANSACTION;
LET $before = (SELECT content, version FROM ONLY type::record($table, $key));
IF $before = NONE { THROW "memory not found" };
UPDATE type::record($table, $key) SET content = $content, version = $before.version + 1;
INSERT INTO memory_history { memory_id: type::record($table, $key), version: $before.version + 1, old_content: $before.content, new_content: $content, change_type: "updated" };
COMMIT TRANSACTION;
SELECT count() FROM memory_history GROUP ALL;
